[stderr] +async () => {
[stderr] +  const workbook = univerAPI.getActiveWorkbook();
[stderr] +  const sheet = workbook.getSheetByName('Sheet1');
[stderr] +  if (!sheet) return { success: false, error: 'Sheet1 not found' };
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const target = sheet.getRange('A1:H85');
[stderr] +  const cellDatas = target.getCellDatas();
[stderr] +  const values = target.getValues();
[stderr] +  const formulas = target.getFormulas();
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const cloneCell = (cell) => {
[stderr] +    if (!cell) return null;
[stderr] +    return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(cell));
[stderr] +  };
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const cleanText = (v) =>
[stderr] +    String(v ?? '').replace(/\u00A0/g, ' ').replace(/\r\n/g, '\n').replace(/\r/g, '\n').trim();
[stderr] +  const isBlankRow = (row) => row.every((cell) => {
[stderr] +    if (!cell) return true;
[stderr] +    if (cell.f) return false;
[stderr] +    if (cell.p?.body?.dataStream) return cleanText(cell.p.body.dataStream) === '';
[stderr] +    return cleanText(cell.v) === '';
[stderr] +  });
[stderr] +  const parseNumberLoose = (v) => {
[stderr] +    const s = cleanText(v).replace(/,/g, '');
[stderr] +    if (s === '') return null;
[stderr] +    const n = Number(s);
[stderr] +    return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null;
[stderr] +  };
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const blocks = [];
[stderr] +  let start = null;
[stderr] +  for (let r = 0; r < 85; r++) {
[stderr] +    const blank = isBlankRow(cellDatas[r]);
[stderr] +    if (!blank && start === null) start = r;
[stderr] +    if ((blank || r === 84) && start !== null) {
[stderr] +      const end = blank ? r - 1 : r;
[stderr] +      blocks.push({ start, end });
[stderr] +      start = null;
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +  }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const out = cellDatas.map((row) => row.map(cloneCell));
[stderr] +  const sortedSummaries = [];
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  for (const block of blocks) {
[stderr] +    const rows = [];
[stderr] +    for (let r = block.start; r <= block.end; r++) {
[stderr] +      rows.push({
[stderr] +        originalIndex: r,
[stderr] +        cells: cellDatas[r].map(cloneCell),
[stderr] +        key: parseNumberLoose(values[r][3]),
[stderr] +      });
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +    rows.sort((a, b) => {
[stderr] +      const ak = a.key;
[stderr] +      const bk = b.key;
[stderr] +      if (ak === null && bk === null) return a.originalIndex - b.originalIndex;
[stderr] +      if (ak === null) return 1;
[stderr] +      if (bk === null) return -1;
[stderr] +      if (bk !== ak) return bk - ak;
[stderr] +      return a.originalIndex - b.originalIndex;
[stderr] +    });
[stderr] +
[stderr] +    for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
[stderr] +      const dest = block.start + i;
[stderr] +      const excelRow = dest + 1;
[stderr] +      const rowCells = rows[i].cells.map(cloneCell);
[stderr] +
[stderr] +      for (let c = 0; c < 8; c++) {
[stderr] +        const cell = rowCells[c];
[stderr] +        if (!cell) continue;
[stderr] +        if (!cell.f && typeof cell.v === 'string' && cleanText(cell.v).startsWith('=')) {
[stderr] +          cell.f = cleanText(cell.v);
[stderr] +          delete cell.v;
[stderr] +          delete cell.t;
[stderr] +        }
[stderr] +      }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +      rowCells[3] = { ...(rowCells[3] || {}), f: `=B${excelRow}+C${excelRow}` };
[stderr] +      rowCells[5] = { ...(rowCells[5] || {}), f: `=D${excelRow}+E${excelRow}` };
[stderr] +      delete rowCells[3].si;
[stderr] +      delete rowCells[5].si;
[stderr] +
[stderr] +      out[dest] = rowCells;
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +    sortedSummaries.push({
[stderr] +      rows: `${block.start + 1}:${block.end + 1}`,
[stderr] +      keys: rows.map((r) => r.key),
[stderr] +    });
[stderr] +  }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  target.setValues(out);
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const numberFormats = Array.from({ length: 85 }, () => ['##0.#', '##0.#', '##0.#']);
[stderr] +  sheet.getRange('D1:F85')
[stderr] +    .setFontWeight('bold')
[stderr] +    .setHorizontalAlignment('center')
[stderr] +    .setNumberFormats(numberFormats);
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  await univerAPI.getFormula().onCalculationResultApplied();
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const after = sheet.getRange('A1:H85');
[stderr] +  const afterValues = after.getValues();
[stderr] +  const afterFormulas = after.getFormulas();
[stderr] +  const afterCells = after.getCellDatas();
[stderr] +  const blockChecks = blocks.map((block) => {
[stderr] +    const keys = [];
[stderr] +    const formulasOk = [];
[stderr] +    for (let r = block.start; r <= block.end; r++) {
[stderr] +      keys.push(parseNumberLoose(afterValues[r][3]));
[stderr] +      formulasOk.push(afterFormulas[r][3] === `=B${r + 1}+C${r + 1}` && afterFormulas[r][5] === `=D${r + 1}+E${r + 1}`);
[stderr] +    }

[stderr] +      formulasOk: formulasOk.every(Boolean),
[stderr] +      keys,
[stderr] +    };

[stderr] +    String(v ?? '').replace(/\u00A0/g, ' ').replace(/\r\n/g, '\n').replace(/\r/g, '\n').trim();
[stderr] +  const isBlankRow = (row) => row.every((cell) => {
[stderr] +    if (!cell) return true;
[stderr] +    if (cell.f) return false;
[stderr] +    if (cell.p?.body?.dataStream) return cleanText(cell.p.body.dataStream) === '';
[stderr] +    return cleanText(cell.v) === '';
[stderr] +  });
[stderr] +  const parseNumberLoose = (v) => {
[stderr] +    const s = cleanText(v).replace(/,/g, '');
[stderr] +    if (s === '') return null;
[stderr] +    const n = Number(s);
[stderr] +    return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null;
[stderr] +  };
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const blocks = [];
[stderr] +  let start = null;
[stderr] +  for (let r = 0; r < 85; r++) {
[stderr] +    const blank = isBlankRow(cellDatas[r]);
[stderr] +    if (!blank && start === null) start = r;
[stderr] +    if ((blank || r === 84) && start !== null) {
[stderr] +      const end = blank ? r - 1 : r;
[stderr] +      blocks.push({ start, end });
[stderr] +      start = null;
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +  }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const out = cellDatas.map((row) => row.map(cloneCell));
[stderr] +  const sortedSummaries = [];
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  for (const block of blocks) {
[stderr] +    const rows = [];
[stderr] +    for (let r = block.start; r <= block.end; r++) {
[stderr] +      rows.push({
[stderr] +        originalIndex: r,
[stderr] +        cells: cellDatas[r].map(cloneCell),
[stderr] +        key: parseNumberLoose(values[r][3]),
[stderr] +      });
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +    rows.sort((a, b) => {
[stderr] +      const ak = a.key;
[stderr] +      const bk = b.key;
[stderr] +      if (ak === null && bk === null) return a.originalIndex - b.originalIndex;
[stderr] +      if (ak === null) return 1;
[stderr] +      if (bk === null) return -1;
[stderr] +      if (bk !== ak) return bk - ak;
[stderr] +      return a.originalIndex - b.originalIndex;
[stderr] +    });
[stderr] +
[stderr] +    for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
[stderr] +      const dest = block.start + i;
[stderr] +      const excelRow = dest + 1;
[stderr] +      const rowCells = rows[i].cells.map(cloneCell);
[stderr] +
[stderr] +      for (let c = 0; c < 8; c++) {
[stderr] +        const cell = rowCells[c];
[stderr] +        if (!cell) continue;
[stderr] +        if (!cell.f && typeof cell.v === 'string' && cleanText(cell.v).startsWith('=')) {
[stderr] +          cell.f = cleanText(cell.v);
[stderr] +          delete cell.v;
[stderr] +          delete cell.t;
[stderr] +        }
[stderr] +      }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +      rowCells[3] = { ...(rowCells[3] || {}), f: `=B${excelRow}+C${excelRow}` };
[stderr] +      rowCells[5] = { ...(rowCells[5] || {}), f: `=D${excelRow}+E${excelRow}` };
[stderr] +      delete rowCells[3].si;
[stderr] +      delete rowCells[5].si;
[stderr] +
[stderr] +      out[dest] = rowCells;
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +    sortedSummaries.push({
[stderr] +      rows: `${block.start + 1}:${block.end + 1}`,
[stderr] +      keys: rows.map((r) => r.key),
[stderr] +    });
[stderr] +  }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  target.setValues(out);
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const numberFormats = Array.from({ length: 85 }, () => ['##0.#', '##0.#', '##0.#']);
[stderr] +  sheet.getRange('D1:F85')
[stderr] +    .setFontWeight('bold')
[stderr] +    .setHorizontalAlignment('center')
[stderr] +    .setNumberFormats(numberFormats);
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  await univerAPI.getFormula().onCalculationResultApplied();
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const after = sheet.getRange('A1:H85');
[stderr] +  const afterValues = after.getValues();
[stderr] +  const afterFormulas = after.getFormulas();
[stderr] +  const afterCells = after.getCellDatas();
[stderr] +  const blockChecks = blocks.map((block) => {
[stderr] +    const keys = [];
[stderr] +    const formulasOk = [];
[stderr] +    for (let r = block.start; r <= block.end; r++) {
[stderr] +      keys.push(parseNumberLoose(afterValues[r][3]));
[stderr] +      formulasOk.push(afterFormulas[r][3] === `=B${r + 1}+C${r + 1}` && afterFormulas[r][5] === `=D${r + 1}+E${r + 1}`);
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +    return {
[stderr] +      rows: `${block.start + 1}:${block.end + 1}`,
[stderr] +      descending: keys.every((v, i) => i === 0 || keys[i - 1] >= v),
[stderr] +      formulasOk: formulasOk.every(Boolean),
[stderr] +      keys,
[stderr] +    };
[stderr] +  });
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const blankRows = [];
[stderr] +  for (let r = 0; r < 85; r++) {
[stderr] +    if (isBlankRow(afterCells[r])) blankRows.push(r + 1);
[stderr] +  }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  return {
[stderr] +    success: true,
[stderr] +    blocks: blocks.map((b) => `${b.start + 1}:${b.end + 1}`),
[stderr] +    blankRows,
[stderr] +    sortedSummaries,
[stderr] +    blockChecks,
[stderr] +    samples: [
[stderr] +      { row: 1, values: afterValues[0], formulas: afterFormulas[0], cells: afterCells[0].slice(3, 6) },
[stderr] +      { row: 8, cells: afterCells[7] },
[stderr] +      { row: 85, values: afterValues[84], formulas: afterFormulas[84], cells: afterCells[84].slice(3, 6) },
[stderr] +    ],
[stderr] +  };
[stderr] +}

[stderr] new file mode 100644
[stderr] index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..c293033ba9c0f7a348aa21441caf312687023c66
[stderr] --- /dev/null
[stderr] +++ b/work/sort_blocks.js
[stderr] @@ -0,0 +1,140 @@
[stderr] +async () => {
[stderr] +  const workbook = univerAPI.getActiveWorkbook();
[stderr] +  const sheet = workbook.getSheetByName('Sheet1');
[stderr] +  if (!sheet) return { success: false, error: 'Sheet1 not found' };
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const target = sheet.getRange('A1:H85');
[stderr] +  const cellDatas = target.getCellDatas();
[stderr] +  const values = target.getValues();
[stderr] +  const formulas = target.getFormulas();
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const cloneCell = (cell) => {
[stderr] +    if (!cell) return null;
[stderr] +    return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(cell));
[stderr] +  };
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const cleanText = (v) =>
[stderr] +    String(v ?? '').replace(/\u00A0/g, ' ').replace(/\r\n/g, '\n').replace(/\r/g, '\n').trim();
[stderr] +  const isBlankRow = (row) => row.every((cell) => {
[stderr] +    if (!cell) return true;
[stderr] +    if (cell.f) return false;
[stderr] +    if (cell.p?.body?.dataStream) return cleanText(cell.p.body.dataStream) === '';
[stderr] +    return cleanText(cell.v) === '';
[stderr] +  });
[stderr] +  const parseNumberLoose = (v) => {
[stderr] +    const s = cleanText(v).replace(/,/g, '');
[stderr] +    if (s === '') return null;
[stderr] +    const n = Number(s);
[stderr] +    return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null;
[stderr] +  };
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const blocks = [];
[stderr] +  let start = null;
[stderr] +  for (let r = 0; r < 85; r++) {
[stderr] +    const blank = isBlankRow(cellDatas[r]);
[stderr] +    if (!blank && start === null) start = r;
[stderr] +    if ((blank || r === 84) && start !== null) {
[stderr] +      const end = blank ? r - 1 : r;
[stderr] +      blocks.push({ start, end });
[stderr] +      start = null;
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +  }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const out = cellDatas.map((row) => row.map(cloneCell));
[stderr] +  const sortedSummaries = [];
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  for (const block of blocks) {
[stderr] +    const rows = [];
[stderr] +    for (let r = block.start; r <= block.end; r++) {
[stderr] +      rows.push({
[stderr] +        originalIndex: r,
[stderr] +        cells: cellDatas[r].map(cloneCell),
[stderr] +        key: parseNumberLoose(values[r][3]),
[stderr] +      });
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +    rows.sort((a, b) => {
[stderr] +      const ak = a.key;
[stderr] +      const bk = b.key;
[stderr] +      if (ak === null && bk === null) return a.originalIndex - b.originalIndex;
[stderr] +      if (ak === null) return 1;
[stderr] +      if (bk === null) return -1;
[stderr] +      if (bk !== ak) return bk - ak;
[stderr] +      return a.originalIndex - b.originalIndex;
[stderr] +    });
[stderr] +
[stderr] +    for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
[stderr] +      const dest = block.start + i;
[stderr] +      const excelRow = dest + 1;
[stderr] +      const rowCells = rows[i].cells.map(cloneCell);
[stderr] +
[stderr] +      for (let c = 0; c < 8; c++) {
[stderr] +        const cell = rowCells[c];
[stderr] +        if (!cell) continue;
[stderr] +        if (!cell.f && typeof cell.v === 'string' && cleanText(cell.v).startsWith('=')) {
[stderr] +          cell.f = cleanText(cell.v);
[stderr] +          delete cell.v;
[stderr] +          delete cell.t;
[stderr] +        }
[stderr] +      }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +      rowCells[3] = { ...(rowCells[3] || {}), f: `=B${excelRow}+C${excelRow}` };
[stderr] +      rowCells[5] = { ...(rowCells[5] || {}), f: `=D${excelRow}+E${excelRow}` };
[stderr] +      delete rowCells[3].si;
[stderr] +      delete rowCells[5].si;
[stderr] +
[stderr] +      out[dest] = rowCells;
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +    sortedSummaries.push({
[stderr] +      rows: `${block.start + 1}:${block.end + 1}`,
[stderr] +      keys: rows.map((r) => r.key),
[stderr] +    });
[stderr] +  }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  target.setValues(out);
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const numberFormats = Array.from({ length: 85 }, () => ['##0.#', '##0.#', '##0.#']);
[stderr] +  sheet.getRange('D1:F85')
[stderr] +    .setFontWeight('bold')
[stderr] +    .setHorizontalAlignment('center')
[stderr] +    .setNumberFormats(numberFormats);
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  await univerAPI.getFormula().onCalculationResultApplied();
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const after = sheet.getRange('A1:H85');
[stderr] +  const afterValues = after.getValues();
[stderr] +  const afterFormulas = after.getFormulas();
[stderr] +  const afterCells = after.getCellDatas();
[stderr] +  const blockChecks = blocks.map((block) => {
[stderr] +    const keys = [];
[stderr] +    const formulasOk = [];
[stderr] +    for (let r = block.start; r <= block.end; r++) {
[stderr] +      keys.push(parseNumberLoose(afterValues[r][3]));
[stderr] +      formulasOk.push(afterFormulas[r][3] === `=B${r + 1}+C${r + 1}` && afterFormulas[r][5] === `=D${r + 1}+E${r + 1}`);
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +    return {
[stderr] +      rows: `${block.start + 1}:${block.end + 1}`,
[stderr] +      descending: keys.every((v, i) => i === 0 || keys[i - 1] >= v),
[stderr] +      formulasOk: formulasOk.every(Boolean),
[stderr] +      keys,
[stderr] +    };
[stderr] +  });
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const blankRows = [];
[stderr] +  for (let r = 0; r < 85; r++) {
[stderr] +    if (isBlankRow(afterCells[r])) blankRows.push(r + 1);
[stderr] +  }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  return {
[stderr] +    success: true,
[stderr] +    blocks: blocks.map((b) => `${b.start + 1}:${b.end + 1}`),
[stderr] +    blankRows,
[stderr] +    sortedSummaries,
[stderr] +    blockChecks,
[stderr] +    samples: [
[stderr] +      { row: 1, values: afterValues[0], formulas: afterFormulas[0], cells: afterCells[0].slice(3, 6) },
[stderr] +      { row: 8, cells: afterCells[7] },
[stderr] +      { row: 85, values: afterValues[84], formulas: afterFormulas[84], cells: afterCells[84].slice(3, 6) },
[stderr] +    ],
[stderr] +  };
[stderr] +}

[stderr] +() => {
[stderr] +  const workbook = univerAPI.getActiveWorkbook();
[stderr] +  const sheet = workbook.getSheetByName('Sheet1');
[stderr] +  if (!sheet) return { success: false, error: 'Sheet1 not found' };
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const range = sheet.getRange('A1:H85');
[stderr] +  const values = range.getValues();
[stderr] +  const formulas = range.getFormulas();
[stderr] +  const cellDatas = range.getCellDatas();
[stderr] +  const cleanText = (v) => String(v ?? '').replace(/\u00A0/g, ' ').trim();
[stderr] +  const parseNumberLoose = (v) => {
[stderr] +    const s = cleanText(v).replace(/,/g, '');
[stderr] +    if (s === '') return null;
[stderr] +    const n = Number(s);
[stderr] +    return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null;
[stderr] +  };
[stderr] +  const isBlankRow = (row) => row.every((cell) => !cell || (!cell.f && cleanText(cell.v) === ''));
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const blocks = [];
[stderr] +  let start = null;
[stderr] +  for (let r = 0; r < 85; r++) {
[stderr] +    const blank = isBlankRow(cellDatas[r]);
[stderr] +    if (!blank && start === null) start = r;
[stderr] +    if ((blank || r === 84) && start !== null) {
[stderr] +      blocks.push({ start, end: blank ? r - 1 : r });
[stderr] +      start = null;
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +  }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const checks = blocks.map((b) => {
[stderr] +    const keys = [];
[stderr] +    const formulaProblems = [];
[stderr] +    const textFormulaProblems = [];
[stderr] +    for (let r = b.start; r <= b.end; r++) {
[stderr] +      keys.push(parseNumberLoose(values[r][3]));
[stderr] +      if (formulas[r][3] !== `=B${r + 1}+C${r + 1}`) formulaProblems.push(`D${r + 1}:${formulas[r][3]}`);
[stderr] +      if (formulas[r][5] !== `=D${r + 1}+E${r + 1}`) formulaProblems.push(`F${r + 1}:${formulas[r][5]}`);
[stderr] +      for (const c of [3, 4, 5]) {
[stderr] +        const cell = cellDatas[r][c];
[stderr] +        if (typeof cell?.v === 'string' && cleanText(cell.v).startsWith('=')) {
[stderr] +          textFormulaProblems.push(`${String.fromCharCode(65 + c)}${r + 1}`);
[stderr] +        }
[stderr] +      }
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +    return {
[stderr] +      rows: `${b.start + 1}:${b.end + 1}`,
[stderr] +      descending: keys.every((v, i) => i === 0 || keys[i - 1] >= v),
[stderr] +      formulaProblems,
[stderr] +      textFormulaProblems,
[stderr] +      keys,
[stderr] +    };
[stderr] +  });
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const display = sheet.getRange('D1:F5').getValues();
[stderr] +  const sampleCells = [0, 1, 7, 8, 84].map((r) => ({
[stderr] +    row: r + 1,
[stderr] +    values: values[r],
[stderr] +    formulas: formulas[r],
[stderr] +    dToF: cellDatas[r].slice(3, 6),
[stderr] +  }));
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  return {
[stderr] +    success: true,
[stderr] +    blocks: blocks.map((b) => `${b.start + 1}:${b.end + 1}`),
[stderr] +    blankRows: cellDatas.map((row, i) => isBlankRow(row) ? i + 1 : null).filter(Boolean),
[stderr] +    allDescending: checks.every((c) => c.descending),
[stderr] +    allFormulasOk: checks.every((c) => c.formulaProblems.length === 0),
[stderr] +    noTextStoredFormulasInDToF: checks.every((c) => c.textFormulaProblems.length === 0),
[stderr] +    checks,
[stderr] +    displayD1F5: display,
[stderr] +    sampleCells,
[stderr] +  };
[stderr] +}

[stderr] +    return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null;
[stderr] +  };

[stderr] +    const keys = [];
[stderr] +    const formulaProblems = [];

[stderr] +async () => {
[stderr] +  const workbook = univerAPI.getActiveWorkbook();
[stderr] +  const sheet = workbook.getSheetByName('Sheet1');
[stderr] +  if (!sheet) return { success: false, error: 'Sheet1 not found' };
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const target = sheet.getRange('A1:H85');
[stderr] +  const cellDatas = target.getCellDatas();
[stderr] +  const values = target.getValues();
[stderr] +  const formulas = target.getFormulas();
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const cloneCell = (cell) => {
[stderr] +    if (!cell) return null;
[stderr] +    return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(cell));
[stderr] +  };
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const cleanText = (v) =>
[stderr] +    String(v ?? '').replace(/\u00A0/g, ' ').replace(/\r\n/g, '\n').replace(/\r/g, '\n').trim();
[stderr] +  const isBlankRow = (row) => row.every((cell) => {
[stderr] +    if (!cell) return true;
[stderr] +    if (cell.f) return false;
[stderr] +    if (cell.p?.body?.dataStream) return cleanText(cell.p.body.dataStream) === '';
[stderr] +    return cleanText(cell.v) === '';
[stderr] +  });
[stderr] +  const parseNumberLoose = (v) => {
[stderr] +    const s = cleanText(v).replace(/,/g, '');
[stderr] +    if (s === '') return null;
[stderr] +    const n = Number(s);
[stderr] +    return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null;
[stderr] +  };
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const blocks = [];
[stderr] +  let start = null;
[stderr] +  for (let r = 0; r < 85; r++) {
[stderr] +    const blank = isBlankRow(cellDatas[r]);
[stderr] +    if (!blank && start === null) start = r;
[stderr] +    if ((blank || r === 84) && start !== null) {
[stderr] +      const end = blank ? r - 1 : r;
[stderr] +      blocks.push({ start, end });
[stderr] +      start = null;
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +  }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const out = cellDatas.map((row) => row.map(cloneCell));
[stderr] +  const sortedSummaries = [];
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  for (const block of blocks) {
[stderr] +    const rows = [];
[stderr] +    for (let r = block.start; r <= block.end; r++) {
[stderr] +      rows.push({
[stderr] +        originalIndex: r,
[stderr] +        cells: cellDatas[r].map(cloneCell),
[stderr] +        key: parseNumberLoose(values[r][3]),
[stderr] +      });
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +    rows.sort((a, b) => {
[stderr] +      const ak = a.key;
[stderr] +      const bk = b.key;
[stderr] +      if (ak === null && bk === null) return a.originalIndex - b.originalIndex;
[stderr] +      if (ak === null) return 1;
[stderr] +      if (bk === null) return -1;
[stderr] +      if (bk !== ak) return bk - ak;
[stderr] +      return a.originalIndex - b.originalIndex;
[stderr] +    });
[stderr] +
[stderr] +    for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
[stderr] +      const dest = block.start + i;
[stderr] +      const excelRow = dest + 1;
[stderr] +      const rowCells = rows[i].cells.map(cloneCell);
[stderr] +
[stderr] +      for (let c = 0; c < 8; c++) {
[stderr] +        const cell = rowCells[c];
[stderr] +        if (!cell) continue;
[stderr] +        if (!cell.f && typeof cell.v === 'string' && cleanText(cell.v).startsWith('=')) {
[stderr] +          cell.f = cleanText(cell.v);
[stderr] +          delete cell.v;
[stderr] +          delete cell.t;
[stderr] +        }
[stderr] +      }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +      rowCells[3] = { ...(rowCells[3] || {}), f: `=B${excelRow}+C${excelRow}` };
[stderr] +      rowCells[5] = { ...(rowCells[5] || {}), f: `=D${excelRow}+E${excelRow}` };
[stderr] +      delete rowCells[3].si;
[stderr] +      delete rowCells[5].si;
[stderr] +
[stderr] +      out[dest] = rowCells;
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +    sortedSummaries.push({
[stderr] +      rows: `${block.start + 1}:${block.end + 1}`,
[stderr] +      keys: rows.map((r) => r.key),
[stderr] +    });
[stderr] +  }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  target.setValues(out);
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const numberFormats = Array.from({ length: 85 }, () => ['##0.#', '##0.#', '##0.#']);
[stderr] +  sheet.getRange('D1:F85')
[stderr] +    .setFontWeight('bold')
[stderr] +    .setHorizontalAlignment('center')
[stderr] +    .setNumberFormats(numberFormats);
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  await univerAPI.getFormula().onCalculationResultApplied();
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const after = sheet.getRange('A1:H85');
[stderr] +  const afterValues = after.getValues();
[stderr] +  const afterFormulas = after.getFormulas();
[stderr] +  const afterCells = after.getCellDatas();
[stderr] +  const blockChecks = blocks.map((block) => {
[stderr] +    const keys = [];
[stderr] +    const formulasOk = [];
[stderr] +    for (let r = block.start; r <= block.end; r++) {
[stderr] +      keys.push(parseNumberLoose(afterValues[r][3]));
[stderr] +      formulasOk.push(afterFormulas[r][3] === `=B${r + 1}+C${r + 1}` && afterFormulas[r][5] === `=D${r + 1}+E${r + 1}`);
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +    return {
[stderr] +      rows: `${block.start + 1}:${block.end + 1}`,
[stderr] +      descending: keys.every((v, i) => i === 0 || keys[i - 1] >= v),
[stderr] +      formulasOk: formulasOk.every(Boolean),
[stderr] +      keys,
[stderr] +    };
[stderr] +  });
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const blankRows = [];
[stderr] +  for (let r = 0; r < 85; r++) {
[stderr] +    if (isBlankRow(afterCells[r])) blankRows.push(r + 1);
[stderr] +  }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  return {
[stderr] +    success: true,
[stderr] +    blocks: blocks.map((b) => `${b.start + 1}:${b.end + 1}`),
[stderr] +    blankRows,
[stderr] +    sortedSummaries,
[stderr] +    blockChecks,
[stderr] +    samples: [
[stderr] +      { row: 1, values: afterValues[0], formulas: afterFormulas[0], cells: afterCells[0].slice(3, 6) },
[stderr] +      { row: 8, cells: afterCells[7] },
[stderr] +      { row: 85, values: afterValues[84], formulas: afterFormulas[84], cells: afterCells[84].slice(3, 6) },
[stderr] +    ],
[stderr] +  };
[stderr] +}

[stderr] +    sortedSummaries.push({
[stderr] +      rows: `${block.start + 1}:${block.end + 1}`,
[stderr] +      keys: rows.map((r) => r.key),
[stderr] +    });
[stderr] +  }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  target.setValues(out);
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const numberFormats = Array.from({ length: 85 }, () => ['##0.#', '##0.#', '##0.#']);
[stderr] +  sheet.getRange('D1:F85')
[stderr] +    .setFontWeight('bold')
[stderr] +    .setHorizontalAlignment('center')
[stderr] +    .setNumberFormats(numberFormats);

[stderr] +  await univerAPI.getFormula().onCalculationResultApplied();

[stderr] +  const after = sheet.getRange('A1:H85');
[stderr] +  const afterValues = after.getValues();
[stderr] +  const afterFormulas = after.getFormulas();
[stderr] +  const afterCells = after.getCellDatas();
[stderr] +  const blockChecks = blocks.map((block) => {
[stderr] +    const keys = [];
[stderr] +    const formulasOk = [];
[stderr] +    for (let r = block.start; r <= block.end; r++) {
[stderr] +      keys.push(parseNumberLoose(afterValues[r][3]));
[stderr] +      formulasOk.push(afterFormulas[r][3] === `=B${r + 1}+C${r + 1}` && afterFormulas[r][5] === `=D${r + 1}+E${r + 1}`);
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +    return {
[stderr] +      rows: `${block.start + 1}:${block.end + 1}`,
[stderr] +      descending: keys.every((v, i) => i === 0 || keys[i - 1] >= v),
[stderr] +      formulasOk: formulasOk.every(Boolean),
[stderr] +      keys,
[stderr] +    };
[stderr] +  });
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const blankRows = [];
[stderr] +  for (let r = 0; r < 85; r++) {
[stderr] +    if (isBlankRow(afterCells[r])) blankRows.push(r + 1);
[stderr] +  }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  return {
[stderr] +    success: true,
[stderr] +    blocks: blocks.map((b) => `${b.start + 1}:${b.end + 1}`),
[stderr] +    blankRows,
[stderr] +    sortedSummaries,
[stderr] +    blockChecks,
[stderr] +    samples: [
[stderr] +      { row: 1, values: afterValues[0], formulas: afterFormulas[0], cells: afterCells[0].slice(3, 6) },
[stderr] +      { row: 8, cells: afterCells[7] },
[stderr] +      { row: 85, values: afterValues[84], formulas: afterFormulas[84], cells: afterCells[84].slice(3, 6) },
[stderr] +    ],
[stderr] +  };
[stderr] +}

[stderr] +() => {
[stderr] +  const workbook = univerAPI.getActiveWorkbook();
[stderr] +  const sheet = workbook.getSheetByName('Sheet1');
[stderr] +  if (!sheet) return { success: false, error: 'Sheet1 not found' };
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const range = sheet.getRange('A1:H85');
[stderr] +  const values = range.getValues();
[stderr] +  const formulas = range.getFormulas();
[stderr] +  const cellDatas = range.getCellDatas();
[stderr] +  const cleanText = (v) => String(v ?? '').replace(/\u00A0/g, ' ').trim();
[stderr] +  const parseNumberLoose = (v) => {
[stderr] +    const s = cleanText(v).replace(/,/g, '');
[stderr] +    if (s === '') return null;
[stderr] +    const n = Number(s);
[stderr] +    return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null;
[stderr] +  };
[stderr] +  const isBlankRow = (row) => row.every((cell) => !cell || (!cell.f && cleanText(cell.v) === ''));
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const blocks = [];
[stderr] +  let start = null;
[stderr] +  for (let r = 0; r < 85; r++) {
[stderr] +    const blank = isBlankRow(cellDatas[r]);
[stderr] +    if (!blank && start === null) start = r;
[stderr] +    if ((blank || r === 84) && start !== null) {
[stderr] +      blocks.push({ start, end: blank ? r - 1 : r });
[stderr] +      start = null;
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +  }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const checks = blocks.map((b) => {
[stderr] +    const keys = [];
[stderr] +    const formulaProblems = [];
[stderr] +    const textFormulaProblems = [];
[stderr] +    for (let r = b.start; r <= b.end; r++) {
[stderr] +      keys.push(parseNumberLoose(values[r][3]));
[stderr] +      if (formulas[r][3] !== `=B${r + 1}+C${r + 1}`) formulaProblems.push(`D${r + 1}:${formulas[r][3]}`);
[stderr] +      if (formulas[r][5] !== `=D${r + 1}+E${r + 1}`) formulaProblems.push(`F${r + 1}:${formulas[r][5]}`);
[stderr] +      for (const c of [3, 4, 5]) {
[stderr] +        const cell = cellDatas[r][c];
[stderr] +        if (typeof cell?.v === 'string' && cleanText(cell.v).startsWith('=')) {
[stderr] +          textFormulaProblems.push(`${String.fromCharCode(65 + c)}${r + 1}`);
[stderr] +        }
[stderr] +      }
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +    return {
[stderr] +      rows: `${b.start + 1}:${b.end + 1}`,
[stderr] +      descending: keys.every((v, i) => i === 0 || keys[i - 1] >= v),
[stderr] +      formulaProblems,
[stderr] +      textFormulaProblems,
[stderr] +      keys,
[stderr] +    };
[stderr] +  });
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const display = sheet.getRange('D1:F5').getValues();
[stderr] +  const sampleCells = [0, 1, 7, 8, 84].map((r) => ({
[stderr] +    row: r + 1,
[stderr] +    values: values[r],
[stderr] +    formulas: formulas[r],
[stderr] +    dToF: cellDatas[r].slice(3, 6),
[stderr] +  }));
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  return {
[stderr] +    success: true,
[stderr] +    blocks: blocks.map((b) => `${b.start + 1}:${b.end + 1}`),
[stderr] +    blankRows: cellDatas.map((row, i) => isBlankRow(row) ? i + 1 : null).filter(Boolean),
[stderr] +    allDescending: checks.every((c) => c.descending),
[stderr] +    allFormulasOk: checks.every((c) => c.formulaProblems.length === 0),
[stderr] +    noTextStoredFormulasInDToF: checks.every((c) => c.textFormulaProblems.length === 0),
[stderr] +    checks,
[stderr] +    displayD1F5: display,
[stderr] +    sampleCells,
[stderr] +  };
[stderr] +}

[stderr] diff --git a/work/sort_blocks.js b/work/sort_blocks.js
[stderr] new file mode 100644
[stderr] index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..c293033ba9c0f7a348aa21441caf312687023c66

[stderr] +++ b/work/sort_blocks.js

[stderr] +async () => {
[stderr] +  const workbook = univerAPI.getActiveWorkbook();
[stderr] +  const sheet = workbook.getSheetByName('Sheet1');
[stderr] +  if (!sheet) return { success: false, error: 'Sheet1 not found' };
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const target = sheet.getRange('A1:H85');
[stderr] +  const cellDatas = target.getCellDatas();
[stderr] +  const values = target.getValues();
[stderr] +  const formulas = target.getFormulas();
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const cloneCell = (cell) => {
[stderr] +    if (!cell) return null;
[stderr] +    return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(cell));
[stderr] +  };
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const cleanText = (v) =>
[stderr] +    String(v ?? '').replace(/\u00A0/g, ' ').replace(/\r\n/g, '\n').replace(/\r/g, '\n').trim();
[stderr] +  const isBlankRow = (row) => row.every((cell) => {
[stderr] +    if (!cell) return true;
[stderr] +    if (cell.f) return false;
[stderr] +    if (cell.p?.body?.dataStream) return cleanText(cell.p.body.dataStream) === '';
[stderr] +    return cleanText(cell.v) === '';
[stderr] +  });
[stderr] +  const parseNumberLoose = (v) => {
[stderr] +    const s = cleanText(v).replace(/,/g, '');
[stderr] +    if (s === '') return null;
[stderr] +    const n = Number(s);
[stderr] +    return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null;
[stderr] +  };
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const blocks = [];
[stderr] +  let start = null;
[stderr] +  for (let r = 0; r < 85; r++) {
[stderr] +    const blank = isBlankRow(cellDatas[r]);
[stderr] +    if (!blank && start === null) start = r;
[stderr] +    if ((blank || r === 84) && start !== null) {
[stderr] +      const end = blank ? r - 1 : r;
[stderr] +      blocks.push({ start, end });
[stderr] +      start = null;
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +  }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const out = cellDatas.map((row) => row.map(cloneCell));
[stderr] +  const sortedSummaries = [];
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  for (const block of blocks) {
[stderr] +    const rows = [];
[stderr] +    for (let r = block.start; r <= block.end; r++) {
[stderr] +      rows.push({
[stderr] +        originalIndex: r,
[stderr] +        cells: cellDatas[r].map(cloneCell),
[stderr] +        key: parseNumberLoose(values[r][3]),
[stderr] +      });
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +    rows.sort((a, b) => {
[stderr] +      const ak = a.key;
[stderr] +      const bk = b.key;
[stderr] +      if (ak === null && bk === null) return a.originalIndex - b.originalIndex;
[stderr] +      if (ak === null) return 1;
[stderr] +      if (bk === null) return -1;
[stderr] +      if (bk !== ak) return bk - ak;
[stderr] +      return a.originalIndex - b.originalIndex;
[stderr] +    });
[stderr] +
[stderr] +    for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
[stderr] +      const dest = block.start + i;
[stderr] +      const excelRow = dest + 1;
[stderr] +      const rowCells = rows[i].cells.map(cloneCell);
[stderr] +
[stderr] +      for (let c = 0; c < 8; c++) {
[stderr] +        const cell = rowCells[c];
[stderr] +        if (!cell) continue;
[stderr] +        if (!cell.f && typeof cell.v === 'string' && cleanText(cell.v).startsWith('=')) {
[stderr] +          cell.f = cleanText(cell.v);
[stderr] +          delete cell.v;
[stderr] +          delete cell.t;
[stderr] +        }
[stderr] +      }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +      rowCells[3] = { ...(rowCells[3] || {}), f: `=B${excelRow}+C${excelRow}` };
[stderr] +      rowCells[5] = { ...(rowCells[5] || {}), f: `=D${excelRow}+E${excelRow}` };
[stderr] +      delete rowCells[3].si;
[stderr] +      delete rowCells[5].si;
[stderr] +
[stderr] +      out[dest] = rowCells;
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +    sortedSummaries.push({
[stderr] +      rows: `${block.start + 1}:${block.end + 1}`,
[stderr] +      keys: rows.map((r) => r.key),
[stderr] +    });
[stderr] +  }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  target.setValues(out);
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const numberFormats = Array.from({ length: 85 }, () => ['##0.#', '##0.#', '##0.#']);
[stderr] +  sheet.getRange('D1:F85')
[stderr] +    .setFontWeight('bold')
[stderr] +    .setHorizontalAlignment('center')
[stderr] +    .setNumberFormats(numberFormats);
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  await univerAPI.getFormula().onCalculationResultApplied();
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const after = sheet.getRange('A1:H85');
[stderr] +  const afterValues = after.getValues();
[stderr] +  const afterFormulas = after.getFormulas();
[stderr] +  const afterCells = after.getCellDatas();
[stderr] +  const blockChecks = blocks.map((block) => {
[stderr] +    const keys = [];
[stderr] +    const formulasOk = [];
[stderr] +    for (let r = block.start; r <= block.end; r++) {
[stderr] +      keys.push(parseNumberLoose(afterValues[r][3]));
[stderr] +      formulasOk.push(afterFormulas[r][3] === `=B${r + 1}+C${r + 1}` && afterFormulas[r][5] === `=D${r + 1}+E${r + 1}`);
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +    return {
[stderr] +      rows: `${block.start + 1}:${block.end + 1}`,
[stderr] +      descending: keys.every((v, i) => i === 0 || keys[i - 1] >= v),
[stderr] +      formulasOk: formulasOk.every(Boolean),
[stderr] +      keys,
[stderr] +    };
[stderr] +  });
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const blankRows = [];
[stderr] +  for (let r = 0; r < 85; r++) {
[stderr] +    if (isBlankRow(afterCells[r])) blankRows.push(r + 1);
[stderr] +  }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  return {
[stderr] +    success: true,
[stderr] +    blocks: blocks.map((b) => `${b.start + 1}:${b.end + 1}`),
[stderr] +    blankRows,
[stderr] +    sortedSummaries,
[stderr] +    blockChecks,
[stderr] +    samples: [
[stderr] +      { row: 1, values: afterValues[0], formulas: afterFormulas[0], cells: afterCells[0].slice(3, 6) },
[stderr] +      { row: 8, cells: afterCells[7] },
[stderr] +      { row: 85, values: afterValues[84], formulas: afterFormulas[84], cells: afterCells[84].slice(3, 6) },
[stderr] +    ],
[stderr] +  };
[stderr] +}

[stderr] +    if (cell.p?.body?.dataStream) return cleanText(cell.p.body.dataStream) === '';
[stderr] +    return cleanText(cell.v) === '';
[stderr] +  });
[stderr] +  const parseNumberLoose = (v) => {
[stderr] +    const s = cleanText(v).replace(/,/g, '');
[stderr] +    if (s === '') return null;
[stderr] +    const n = Number(s);
[stderr] +    return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null;
[stderr] +  };
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const blocks = [];
[stderr] +  let start = null;
[stderr] +  for (let r = 0; r < 85; r++) {

[stderr] +      rows.push({
[stderr] +        originalIndex: r,
[stderr] +        cells: cellDatas[r].map(cloneCell),
[stderr] +        key: parseNumberLoose(values[r][3]),
[stderr] +      });
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +    rows.sort((a, b) => {
[stderr] +      const ak = a.key;
[stderr] +      const bk = b.key;
[stderr] +      if (ak === null && bk === null) return a.originalIndex - b.originalIndex;
[stderr] +      if (ak === null) return 1;
[stderr] +      if (bk === null) return -1;

[stderr] +      keys,

[stderr] diff --git a/work/sort_blocks.js b/work/sort_blocks.js
[stderr] new file mode 100644
[stderr] index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..c293033ba9c0f7a348aa21441caf312687023c66
[stderr] --- /dev/null
[stderr] +++ b/work/sort_blocks.js
[stderr] @@ -0,0 +1,140 @@
[stderr] +async () => {
[stderr] +  const workbook = univerAPI.getActiveWorkbook();
[stderr] +  const sheet = workbook.getSheetByName('Sheet1');
[stderr] +  if (!sheet) return { success: false, error: 'Sheet1 not found' };
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const target = sheet.getRange('A1:H85');
[stderr] +  const cellDatas = target.getCellDatas();
[stderr] +  const values = target.getValues();
[stderr] +  const formulas = target.getFormulas();
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const cloneCell = (cell) => {
[stderr] +    if (!cell) return null;
[stderr] +    return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(cell));
[stderr] +  };
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const cleanText = (v) =>
[stderr] +    String(v ?? '').replace(/\u00A0/g, ' ').replace(/\r\n/g, '\n').replace(/\r/g, '\n').trim();
[stderr] +  const isBlankRow = (row) => row.every((cell) => {
[stderr] +    if (!cell) return true;
[stderr] +    if (cell.f) return false;
[stderr] +    if (cell.p?.body?.dataStream) return cleanText(cell.p.body.dataStream) === '';
[stderr] +    return cleanText(cell.v) === '';
[stderr] +  });
[stderr] +  const parseNumberLoose = (v) => {
[stderr] +    const s = cleanText(v).replace(/,/g, '');
[stderr] +    if (s === '') return null;
[stderr] +    const n = Number(s);
[stderr] +    return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null;
[stderr] +  };
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const blocks = [];
[stderr] +  let start = null;
[stderr] +  for (let r = 0; r < 85; r++) {
[stderr] +    const blank = isBlankRow(cellDatas[r]);
[stderr] +    if (!blank && start === null) start = r;
[stderr] +    if ((blank || r === 84) && start !== null) {
[stderr] +      const end = blank ? r - 1 : r;
[stderr] +      blocks.push({ start, end });
[stderr] +      start = null;
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +  }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const out = cellDatas.map((row) => row.map(cloneCell));
[stderr] +  const sortedSummaries = [];
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  for (const block of blocks) {
[stderr] +    const rows = [];
[stderr] +    for (let r = block.start; r <= block.end; r++) {
[stderr] +      rows.push({
[stderr] +        originalIndex: r,
[stderr] +        cells: cellDatas[r].map(cloneCell),
[stderr] +        key: parseNumberLoose(values[r][3]),
[stderr] +      });
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +    rows.sort((a, b) => {
[stderr] +      const ak = a.key;
[stderr] +      const bk = b.key;
[stderr] +      if (ak === null && bk === null) return a.originalIndex - b.originalIndex;
[stderr] +      if (ak === null) return 1;
[stderr] +      if (bk === null) return -1;
[stderr] +      if (bk !== ak) return bk - ak;
[stderr] +      return a.originalIndex - b.originalIndex;
[stderr] +    });
[stderr] +
[stderr] +    for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
[stderr] +      const dest = block.start + i;
[stderr] +      const excelRow = dest + 1;
[stderr] +      const rowCells = rows[i].cells.map(cloneCell);
[stderr] +
[stderr] +      for (let c = 0; c < 8; c++) {
[stderr] +        const cell = rowCells[c];
[stderr] +        if (!cell) continue;
[stderr] +        if (!cell.f && typeof cell.v === 'string' && cleanText(cell.v).startsWith('=')) {
[stderr] +          cell.f = cleanText(cell.v);
[stderr] +          delete cell.v;
[stderr] +          delete cell.t;
[stderr] +        }
[stderr] +      }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +      rowCells[3] = { ...(rowCells[3] || {}), f: `=B${excelRow}+C${excelRow}` };
[stderr] +      rowCells[5] = { ...(rowCells[5] || {}), f: `=D${excelRow}+E${excelRow}` };
[stderr] +      delete rowCells[3].si;
[stderr] +      delete rowCells[5].si;
[stderr] +
[stderr] +      out[dest] = rowCells;
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +    sortedSummaries.push({
[stderr] +      rows: `${block.start + 1}:${block.end + 1}`,
[stderr] +      keys: rows.map((r) => r.key),
[stderr] +    });
[stderr] +  }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  target.setValues(out);
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const numberFormats = Array.from({ length: 85 }, () => ['##0.#', '##0.#', '##0.#']);
[stderr] +  sheet.getRange('D1:F85')
[stderr] +    .setFontWeight('bold')
[stderr] +    .setHorizontalAlignment('center')
[stderr] +    .setNumberFormats(numberFormats);
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  await univerAPI.getFormula().onCalculationResultApplied();
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const after = sheet.getRange('A1:H85');
[stderr] +  const afterValues = after.getValues();
[stderr] +  const afterFormulas = after.getFormulas();
[stderr] +  const afterCells = after.getCellDatas();
[stderr] +  const blockChecks = blocks.map((block) => {
[stderr] +    const keys = [];
[stderr] +    const formulasOk = [];
[stderr] +    for (let r = block.start; r <= block.end; r++) {
[stderr] +      keys.push(parseNumberLoose(afterValues[r][3]));
[stderr] +      formulasOk.push(afterFormulas[r][3] === `=B${r + 1}+C${r + 1}` && afterFormulas[r][5] === `=D${r + 1}+E${r + 1}`);
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +    return {
[stderr] +      rows: `${block.start + 1}:${block.end + 1}`,
[stderr] +      descending: keys.every((v, i) => i === 0 || keys[i - 1] >= v),
[stderr] +      formulasOk: formulasOk.every(Boolean),
[stderr] +      keys,
[stderr] +    };
[stderr] +  });
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const blankRows = [];
[stderr] +  for (let r = 0; r < 85; r++) {
[stderr] +    if (isBlankRow(afterCells[r])) blankRows.push(r + 1);
[stderr] +  }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  return {
[stderr] +    success: true,
[stderr] +    blocks: blocks.map((b) => `${b.start + 1}:${b.end + 1}`),
[stderr] +    blankRows,
[stderr] +    sortedSummaries,
[stderr] +    blockChecks,
[stderr] +    samples: [
[stderr] +      { row: 1, values: afterValues[0], formulas: afterFormulas[0], cells: afterCells[0].slice(3, 6) },
[stderr] +      { row: 8, cells: afterCells[7] },
[stderr] +      { row: 85, values: afterValues[84], formulas: afterFormulas[84], cells: afterCells[84].slice(3, 6) },
[stderr] +    ],
[stderr] +  };
[stderr] +}

[stderr] +() => {
[stderr] +  const workbook = univerAPI.getActiveWorkbook();
[stderr] +  const sheet = workbook.getSheetByName('Sheet1');
[stderr] +  if (!sheet) return { success: false, error: 'Sheet1 not found' };
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const range = sheet.getRange('A1:H85');
[stderr] +  const values = range.getValues();
[stderr] +  const formulas = range.getFormulas();
[stderr] +  const cellDatas = range.getCellDatas();
[stderr] +  const cleanText = (v) => String(v ?? '').replace(/\u00A0/g, ' ').trim();
[stderr] +  const parseNumberLoose = (v) => {
[stderr] +    const s = cleanText(v).replace(/,/g, '');
[stderr] +    if (s === '') return null;
[stderr] +    const n = Number(s);
[stderr] +    return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null;
[stderr] +  };
[stderr] +  const isBlankRow = (row) => row.every((cell) => !cell || (!cell.f && cleanText(cell.v) === ''));
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const blocks = [];
[stderr] +  let start = null;
[stderr] +  for (let r = 0; r < 85; r++) {
[stderr] +    const blank = isBlankRow(cellDatas[r]);
[stderr] +    if (!blank && start === null) start = r;
[stderr] +    if ((blank || r === 84) && start !== null) {
[stderr] +      blocks.push({ start, end: blank ? r - 1 : r });
[stderr] +      start = null;
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +  }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const checks = blocks.map((b) => {
[stderr] +    const keys = [];
[stderr] +    const formulaProblems = [];
[stderr] +    const textFormulaProblems = [];
[stderr] +    for (let r = b.start; r <= b.end; r++) {
[stderr] +      keys.push(parseNumberLoose(values[r][3]));
[stderr] +      if (formulas[r][3] !== `=B${r + 1}+C${r + 1}`) formulaProblems.push(`D${r + 1}:${formulas[r][3]}`);
[stderr] +      if (formulas[r][5] !== `=D${r + 1}+E${r + 1}`) formulaProblems.push(`F${r + 1}:${formulas[r][5]}`);
[stderr] +      for (const c of [3, 4, 5]) {
[stderr] +        const cell = cellDatas[r][c];
[stderr] +        if (typeof cell?.v === 'string' && cleanText(cell.v).startsWith('=')) {
[stderr] +          textFormulaProblems.push(`${String.fromCharCode(65 + c)}${r + 1}`);
[stderr] +        }
[stderr] +      }
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +    return {
[stderr] +      rows: `${b.start + 1}:${b.end + 1}`,
[stderr] +      descending: keys.every((v, i) => i === 0 || keys[i - 1] >= v),
[stderr] +      formulaProblems,
[stderr] +      textFormulaProblems,
[stderr] +      keys,
[stderr] +    };
[stderr] +  });
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const display = sheet.getRange('D1:F5').getValues();
[stderr] +  const sampleCells = [0, 1, 7, 8, 84].map((r) => ({
[stderr] +    row: r + 1,
[stderr] +    values: values[r],
[stderr] +    formulas: formulas[r],
[stderr] +    dToF: cellDatas[r].slice(3, 6),
[stderr] +  }));
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  return {
[stderr] +    success: true,
[stderr] +    blocks: blocks.map((b) => `${b.start + 1}:${b.end + 1}`),
[stderr] +    blankRows: cellDatas.map((row, i) => isBlankRow(row) ? i + 1 : null).filter(Boolean),
[stderr] +    allDescending: checks.every((c) => c.descending),
[stderr] +    allFormulasOk: checks.every((c) => c.formulaProblems.length === 0),
[stderr] +    noTextStoredFormulasInDToF: checks.every((c) => c.textFormulaProblems.length === 0),
[stderr] +    checks,
[stderr] +    displayD1F5: display,
[stderr] +    sampleCells,
[stderr] +  };
[stderr] +}

[stderr] +    return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null;

[stderr] +        const cell = cellDatas[r][c];

[stderr] +        }
[stderr] +      }

[stderr] +      rows: `${b.start + 1}:${b.end + 1}`,
[stderr] +      descending: keys.every((v, i) => i === 0 || keys[i - 1] >= v),
[stderr] +      formulaProblems,

[stderr] diff --git a/work/sort_blocks.js b/work/sort_blocks.js
[stderr] new file mode 100644
[stderr] index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..c293033ba9c0f7a348aa21441caf312687023c66
[stderr] --- /dev/null
[stderr] +++ b/work/sort_blocks.js
[stderr] @@ -0,0 +1,140 @@
[stderr] +async () => {
[stderr] +  const workbook = univerAPI.getActiveWorkbook();
[stderr] +  const sheet = workbook.getSheetByName('Sheet1');
[stderr] +  if (!sheet) return { success: false, error: 'Sheet1 not found' };
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const target = sheet.getRange('A1:H85');
[stderr] +  const cellDatas = target.getCellDatas();
[stderr] +  const values = target.getValues();
[stderr] +  const formulas = target.getFormulas();
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const cloneCell = (cell) => {
[stderr] +    if (!cell) return null;
[stderr] +    return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(cell));
[stderr] +  };
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const cleanText = (v) =>
[stderr] +    String(v ?? '').replace(/\u00A0/g, ' ').replace(/\r\n/g, '\n').replace(/\r/g, '\n').trim();
[stderr] +  const isBlankRow = (row) => row.every((cell) => {
[stderr] +    if (!cell) return true;
[stderr] +    if (cell.f) return false;
[stderr] +    if (cell.p?.body?.dataStream) return cleanText(cell.p.body.dataStream) === '';
[stderr] +    return cleanText(cell.v) === '';
[stderr] +  });
[stderr] +  const parseNumberLoose = (v) => {
[stderr] +    const s = cleanText(v).replace(/,/g, '');
[stderr] +    if (s === '') return null;
[stderr] +    const n = Number(s);
[stderr] +    return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null;
[stderr] +  };
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const blocks = [];
[stderr] +  let start = null;
[stderr] +  for (let r = 0; r < 85; r++) {
[stderr] +    const blank = isBlankRow(cellDatas[r]);
[stderr] +    if (!blank && start === null) start = r;
[stderr] +    if ((blank || r === 84) && start !== null) {
[stderr] +      const end = blank ? r - 1 : r;
[stderr] +      blocks.push({ start, end });
[stderr] +      start = null;
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +  }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const out = cellDatas.map((row) => row.map(cloneCell));
[stderr] +  const sortedSummaries = [];
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  for (const block of blocks) {
[stderr] +    const rows = [];
[stderr] +    for (let r = block.start; r <= block.end; r++) {
[stderr] +      rows.push({
[stderr] +        originalIndex: r,
[stderr] +        cells: cellDatas[r].map(cloneCell),
[stderr] +        key: parseNumberLoose(values[r][3]),
[stderr] +      });
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +    rows.sort((a, b) => {
[stderr] +      const ak = a.key;
[stderr] +      const bk = b.key;
[stderr] +      if (ak === null && bk === null) return a.originalIndex - b.originalIndex;
[stderr] +      if (ak === null) return 1;
[stderr] +      if (bk === null) return -1;
[stderr] +      if (bk !== ak) return bk - ak;
[stderr] +      return a.originalIndex - b.originalIndex;
[stderr] +    });
[stderr] +
[stderr] +    for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
[stderr] +      const dest = block.start + i;
[stderr] +      const excelRow = dest + 1;
[stderr] +      const rowCells = rows[i].cells.map(cloneCell);
[stderr] +
[stderr] +      for (let c = 0; c < 8; c++) {
[stderr] +        const cell = rowCells[c];
[stderr] +        if (!cell) continue;
[stderr] +        if (!cell.f && typeof cell.v === 'string' && cleanText(cell.v).startsWith('=')) {
[stderr] +          cell.f = cleanText(cell.v);
[stderr] +          delete cell.v;
[stderr] +          delete cell.t;
[stderr] +        }
[stderr] +      }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +      rowCells[3] = { ...(rowCells[3] || {}), f: `=B${excelRow}+C${excelRow}` };
[stderr] +      rowCells[5] = { ...(rowCells[5] || {}), f: `=D${excelRow}+E${excelRow}` };
[stderr] +      delete rowCells[3].si;
[stderr] +      delete rowCells[5].si;
[stderr] +
[stderr] +      out[dest] = rowCells;
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +    sortedSummaries.push({
[stderr] +      rows: `${block.start + 1}:${block.end + 1}`,
[stderr] +      keys: rows.map((r) => r.key),
[stderr] +    });
[stderr] +  }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  target.setValues(out);
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const numberFormats = Array.from({ length: 85 }, () => ['##0.#', '##0.#', '##0.#']);
[stderr] +  sheet.getRange('D1:F85')
[stderr] +    .setFontWeight('bold')
[stderr] +    .setHorizontalAlignment('center')
[stderr] +    .setNumberFormats(numberFormats);
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  await univerAPI.getFormula().onCalculationResultApplied();
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const after = sheet.getRange('A1:H85');
[stderr] +  const afterValues = after.getValues();
[stderr] +  const afterFormulas = after.getFormulas();
[stderr] +  const afterCells = after.getCellDatas();
[stderr] +  const blockChecks = blocks.map((block) => {
[stderr] +    const keys = [];
[stderr] +    const formulasOk = [];
[stderr] +    for (let r = block.start; r <= block.end; r++) {
[stderr] +      keys.push(parseNumberLoose(afterValues[r][3]));
[stderr] +      formulasOk.push(afterFormulas[r][3] === `=B${r + 1}+C${r + 1}` && afterFormulas[r][5] === `=D${r + 1}+E${r + 1}`);
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +    return {
[stderr] +      rows: `${block.start + 1}:${block.end + 1}`,
[stderr] +      descending: keys.every((v, i) => i === 0 || keys[i - 1] >= v),
[stderr] +      formulasOk: formulasOk.every(Boolean),
[stderr] +      keys,
[stderr] +    };
[stderr] +  });
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const blankRows = [];
[stderr] +  for (let r = 0; r < 85; r++) {
[stderr] +    if (isBlankRow(afterCells[r])) blankRows.push(r + 1);
[stderr] +  }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  return {
[stderr] +    success: true,
[stderr] +    blocks: blocks.map((b) => `${b.start + 1}:${b.end + 1}`),
[stderr] +    blankRows,
[stderr] +    sortedSummaries,
[stderr] +    blockChecks,
[stderr] +    samples: [
[stderr] +      { row: 1, values: afterValues[0], formulas: afterFormulas[0], cells: afterCells[0].slice(3, 6) },
[stderr] +      { row: 8, cells: afterCells[7] },
[stderr] +      { row: 85, values: afterValues[84], formulas: afterFormulas[84], cells: afterCells[84].slice(3, 6) },
[stderr] +    ],
[stderr] +  };
[stderr] +}

[stderr] @@ -0,0 +1,140 @@
[stderr] +async () => {
[stderr] +  const workbook = univerAPI.getActiveWorkbook();
[stderr] +  const sheet = workbook.getSheetByName('Sheet1');
[stderr] +  if (!sheet) return { success: false, error: 'Sheet1 not found' };
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const target = sheet.getRange('A1:H85');
[stderr] +  const cellDatas = target.getCellDatas();
[stderr] +  const values = target.getValues();
[stderr] +  const formulas = target.getFormulas();
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const cloneCell = (cell) => {
[stderr] +    if (!cell) return null;
[stderr] +    return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(cell));
[stderr] +  };
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const cleanText = (v) =>
[stderr] +    String(v ?? '').replace(/\u00A0/g, ' ').replace(/\r\n/g, '\n').replace(/\r/g, '\n').trim();
[stderr] +  const isBlankRow = (row) => row.every((cell) => {
[stderr] +    if (!cell) return true;
[stderr] +    if (cell.f) return false;
[stderr] +    if (cell.p?.body?.dataStream) return cleanText(cell.p.body.dataStream) === '';
[stderr] +    return cleanText(cell.v) === '';
[stderr] +  });
[stderr] +  const parseNumberLoose = (v) => {
[stderr] +    const s = cleanText(v).replace(/,/g, '');
[stderr] +    if (s === '') return null;
[stderr] +    const n = Number(s);
[stderr] +    return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null;
[stderr] +  };
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const blocks = [];
[stderr] +  let start = null;
[stderr] +  for (let r = 0; r < 85; r++) {
[stderr] +    const blank = isBlankRow(cellDatas[r]);
[stderr] +    if (!blank && start === null) start = r;
[stderr] +    if ((blank || r === 84) && start !== null) {
[stderr] +      const end = blank ? r - 1 : r;
[stderr] +      blocks.push({ start, end });
[stderr] +      start = null;
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +  }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const out = cellDatas.map((row) => row.map(cloneCell));
[stderr] +  const sortedSummaries = [];
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  for (const block of blocks) {
[stderr] +    const rows = [];
[stderr] +    for (let r = block.start; r <= block.end; r++) {
[stderr] +      rows.push({
[stderr] +        originalIndex: r,
[stderr] +        cells: cellDatas[r].map(cloneCell),
[stderr] +        key: parseNumberLoose(values[r][3]),
[stderr] +      });
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +    rows.sort((a, b) => {
[stderr] +      const ak = a.key;
[stderr] +      const bk = b.key;
[stderr] +      if (ak === null && bk === null) return a.originalIndex - b.originalIndex;
[stderr] +      if (ak === null) return 1;
[stderr] +      if (bk === null) return -1;
[stderr] +      if (bk !== ak) return bk - ak;
[stderr] +      return a.originalIndex - b.originalIndex;
[stderr] +    });
[stderr] +
[stderr] +    for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
[stderr] +      const dest = block.start + i;
[stderr] +      const excelRow = dest + 1;
[stderr] +      const rowCells = rows[i].cells.map(cloneCell);
[stderr] +
[stderr] +      for (let c = 0; c < 8; c++) {
[stderr] +        const cell = rowCells[c];
[stderr] +        if (!cell) continue;
[stderr] +        if (!cell.f && typeof cell.v === 'string' && cleanText(cell.v).startsWith('=')) {
[stderr] +          cell.f = cleanText(cell.v);
[stderr] +          delete cell.v;
[stderr] +          delete cell.t;
[stderr] +        }
[stderr] +      }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +      rowCells[3] = { ...(rowCells[3] || {}), f: `=B${excelRow}+C${excelRow}` };
[stderr] +      rowCells[5] = { ...(rowCells[5] || {}), f: `=D${excelRow}+E${excelRow}` };
[stderr] +      delete rowCells[3].si;
[stderr] +      delete rowCells[5].si;
[stderr] +
[stderr] +      out[dest] = rowCells;
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +    sortedSummaries.push({
[stderr] +      rows: `${block.start + 1}:${block.end + 1}`,
[stderr] +      keys: rows.map((r) => r.key),
[stderr] +    });
[stderr] +  }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  target.setValues(out);
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const numberFormats = Array.from({ length: 85 }, () => ['##0.#', '##0.#', '##0.#']);
[stderr] +  sheet.getRange('D1:F85')
[stderr] +    .setFontWeight('bold')
[stderr] +    .setHorizontalAlignment('center')
[stderr] +    .setNumberFormats(numberFormats);
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  await univerAPI.getFormula().onCalculationResultApplied();
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const after = sheet.getRange('A1:H85');
[stderr] +  const afterValues = after.getValues();
[stderr] +  const afterFormulas = after.getFormulas();
[stderr] +  const afterCells = after.getCellDatas();
[stderr] +  const blockChecks = blocks.map((block) => {
[stderr] +    const keys = [];
[stderr] +    const formulasOk = [];
[stderr] +    for (let r = block.start; r <= block.end; r++) {
[stderr] +      keys.push(parseNumberLoose(afterValues[r][3]));
[stderr] +      formulasOk.push(afterFormulas[r][3] === `=B${r + 1}+C${r + 1}` && afterFormulas[r][5] === `=D${r + 1}+E${r + 1}`);
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +    return {
[stderr] +      rows: `${block.start + 1}:${block.end + 1}`,
[stderr] +      descending: keys.every((v, i) => i === 0 || keys[i - 1] >= v),
[stderr] +      formulasOk: formulasOk.every(Boolean),
[stderr] +      keys,
[stderr] +    };
[stderr] +  });
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const blankRows = [];
[stderr] +  for (let r = 0; r < 85; r++) {
[stderr] +    if (isBlankRow(afterCells[r])) blankRows.push(r + 1);
[stderr] +  }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  return {
[stderr] +    success: true,
[stderr] +    blocks: blocks.map((b) => `${b.start + 1}:${b.end + 1}`),
[stderr] +    blankRows,
[stderr] +    sortedSummaries,
[stderr] +    blockChecks,
[stderr] +    samples: [
[stderr] +      { row: 1, values: afterValues[0], formulas: afterFormulas[0], cells: afterCells[0].slice(3, 6) },
[stderr] +      { row: 8, cells: afterCells[7] },
[stderr] +      { row: 85, values: afterValues[84], formulas: afterFormulas[84], cells: afterCells[84].slice(3, 6) },
[stderr] +    ],
[stderr] +  };
[stderr] +}

[stderr] +    return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null;

[stderr] +      start = null;
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +  }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const checks = blocks.map((b) => {
[stderr] +    const keys = [];
[stderr] +    const formulaProblems = [];
[stderr] +    const textFormulaProblems = [];
[stderr] +    for (let r = b.start; r <= b.end; r++) {
[stderr] +      keys.push(parseNumberLoose(values[r][3]));
[stderr] +      if (formulas[r][3] !== `=B${r + 1}+C${r + 1}`) formulaProblems.push(`D${r + 1}:${formulas[r][3]}`);
[stderr] +      if (formulas[r][5] !== `=D${r + 1}+E${r + 1}`) formulaProblems.push(`F${r + 1}:${formulas[r][5]}`);
[stderr] +      for (const c of [3, 4, 5]) {
[stderr] +        const cell = cellDatas[r][c];
[stderr] +        if (typeof cell?.v === 'string' && cleanText(cell.v).startsWith('=')) {
[stderr] +          textFormulaProblems.push(`${String.fromCharCode(65 + c)}${r + 1}`);
[stderr] +        }
[stderr] +      }
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +    return {
[stderr] +      rows: `${b.start + 1}:${b.end + 1}`,
[stderr] +      descending: keys.every((v, i) => i === 0 || keys[i - 1] >= v),
[stderr] +      formulaProblems,
[stderr] +      textFormulaProblems,
[stderr] +      keys,
[stderr] +    };
[stderr] +  });
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const display = sheet.getRange('D1:F5').getValues();
[stderr] +  const sampleCells = [0, 1, 7, 8, 84].map((r) => ({
[stderr] +    row: r + 1,
[stderr] +    values: values[r],
[stderr] +    formulas: formulas[r],
[stderr] +    dToF: cellDatas[r].slice(3, 6),
[stderr] +  }));
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  return {
[stderr] +    success: true,
[stderr] +    blocks: blocks.map((b) => `${b.start + 1}:${b.end + 1}`),
[stderr] +    blankRows: cellDatas.map((row, i) => isBlankRow(row) ? i + 1 : null).filter(Boolean),
[stderr] +    allDescending: checks.every((c) => c.descending),
[stderr] +    allFormulasOk: checks.every((c) => c.formulaProblems.length === 0),
[stderr] +    noTextStoredFormulasInDToF: checks.every((c) => c.textFormulaProblems.length === 0),
[stderr] +    checks,
[stderr] +    displayD1F5: display,
[stderr] +    sampleCells,
[stderr] +  };

[stderr] +async () => {
[stderr] +  const workbook = univerAPI.getActiveWorkbook();
[stderr] +  const sheet = workbook.getSheetByName('Sheet1');
[stderr] +  if (!sheet) return { success: false, error: 'Sheet1 not found' };
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const target = sheet.getRange('A1:H85');
[stderr] +  const cellDatas = target.getCellDatas();
[stderr] +  const values = target.getValues();
[stderr] +  const formulas = target.getFormulas();
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const cloneCell = (cell) => {
[stderr] +    if (!cell) return null;
[stderr] +    return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(cell));
[stderr] +  };
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const cleanText = (v) =>
[stderr] +    String(v ?? '').replace(/\u00A0/g, ' ').replace(/\r\n/g, '\n').replace(/\r/g, '\n').trim();
[stderr] +  const isBlankRow = (row) => row.every((cell) => {
[stderr] +    if (!cell) return true;
[stderr] +    if (cell.f) return false;
[stderr] +    if (cell.p?.body?.dataStream) return cleanText(cell.p.body.dataStream) === '';
[stderr] +    return cleanText(cell.v) === '';
[stderr] +  });
[stderr] +  const parseNumberLoose = (v) => {
[stderr] +    const s = cleanText(v).replace(/,/g, '');
[stderr] +    if (s === '') return null;
[stderr] +    const n = Number(s);
[stderr] +    return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null;
[stderr] +  };
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const blocks = [];
[stderr] +  let start = null;
[stderr] +  for (let r = 0; r < 85; r++) {
[stderr] +    const blank = isBlankRow(cellDatas[r]);
[stderr] +    if (!blank && start === null) start = r;
[stderr] +    if ((blank || r === 84) && start !== null) {
[stderr] +      const end = blank ? r - 1 : r;
[stderr] +      blocks.push({ start, end });
[stderr] +      start = null;
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +  }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const out = cellDatas.map((row) => row.map(cloneCell));
[stderr] +  const sortedSummaries = [];
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  for (const block of blocks) {
[stderr] +    const rows = [];
[stderr] +    for (let r = block.start; r <= block.end; r++) {
[stderr] +      rows.push({
[stderr] +        originalIndex: r,
[stderr] +        cells: cellDatas[r].map(cloneCell),
[stderr] +        key: parseNumberLoose(values[r][3]),
[stderr] +      });
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +    rows.sort((a, b) => {
[stderr] +      const ak = a.key;
[stderr] +      const bk = b.key;
[stderr] +      if (ak === null && bk === null) return a.originalIndex - b.originalIndex;
[stderr] +      if (ak === null) return 1;
[stderr] +      if (bk === null) return -1;
[stderr] +      if (bk !== ak) return bk - ak;
[stderr] +      return a.originalIndex - b.originalIndex;
[stderr] +    });
[stderr] +
[stderr] +    for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
[stderr] +      const dest = block.start + i;
[stderr] +      const excelRow = dest + 1;
[stderr] +      const rowCells = rows[i].cells.map(cloneCell);
[stderr] +
[stderr] +      for (let c = 0; c < 8; c++) {
[stderr] +        const cell = rowCells[c];
[stderr] +        if (!cell) continue;
[stderr] +        if (!cell.f && typeof cell.v === 'string' && cleanText(cell.v).startsWith('=')) {
[stderr] +          cell.f = cleanText(cell.v);
[stderr] +          delete cell.v;
[stderr] +          delete cell.t;
[stderr] +        }
[stderr] +      }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +      rowCells[3] = { ...(rowCells[3] || {}), f: `=B${excelRow}+C${excelRow}` };
[stderr] +      rowCells[5] = { ...(rowCells[5] || {}), f: `=D${excelRow}+E${excelRow}` };
[stderr] +      delete rowCells[3].si;
[stderr] +      delete rowCells[5].si;
[stderr] +
[stderr] +      out[dest] = rowCells;
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +    sortedSummaries.push({
[stderr] +      rows: `${block.start + 1}:${block.end + 1}`,
[stderr] +      keys: rows.map((r) => r.key),
[stderr] +    });
[stderr] +  }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  target.setValues(out);
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const numberFormats = Array.from({ length: 85 }, () => ['##0.#', '##0.#', '##0.#']);
[stderr] +  sheet.getRange('D1:F85')
[stderr] +    .setFontWeight('bold')
[stderr] +    .setHorizontalAlignment('center')
[stderr] +    .setNumberFormats(numberFormats);
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  await univerAPI.getFormula().onCalculationResultApplied();
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const after = sheet.getRange('A1:H85');
[stderr] +  const afterValues = after.getValues();
[stderr] +  const afterFormulas = after.getFormulas();
[stderr] +  const afterCells = after.getCellDatas();
[stderr] +  const blockChecks = blocks.map((block) => {
[stderr] +    const keys = [];
[stderr] +    const formulasOk = [];
[stderr] +    for (let r = block.start; r <= block.end; r++) {
[stderr] +      keys.push(parseNumberLoose(afterValues[r][3]));
[stderr] +      formulasOk.push(afterFormulas[r][3] === `=B${r + 1}+C${r + 1}` && afterFormulas[r][5] === `=D${r + 1}+E${r + 1}`);
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +    return {
[stderr] +      rows: `${block.start + 1}:${block.end + 1}`,
[stderr] +      descending: keys.every((v, i) => i === 0 || keys[i - 1] >= v),
[stderr] +      formulasOk: formulasOk.every(Boolean),
[stderr] +      keys,
[stderr] +    };
[stderr] +  });
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const blankRows = [];
[stderr] +  for (let r = 0; r < 85; r++) {
[stderr] +    if (isBlankRow(afterCells[r])) blankRows.push(r + 1);
[stderr] +  }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  return {
[stderr] +    success: true,
[stderr] +    blocks: blocks.map((b) => `${b.start + 1}:${b.end + 1}`),
[stderr] +    blankRows,
[stderr] +    sortedSummaries,
[stderr] +    blockChecks,
[stderr] +    samples: [
[stderr] +      { row: 1, values: afterValues[0], formulas: afterFormulas[0], cells: afterCells[0].slice(3, 6) },
[stderr] +      { row: 8, cells: afterCells[7] },
[stderr] +      { row: 85, values: afterValues[84], formulas: afterFormulas[84], cells: afterCells[84].slice(3, 6) },
[stderr] +    ],
[stderr] +  };
[stderr] +}

[stderr] +    [{ v: 230, t: 2 }, { v: 224.9, t: 2 }, { v: 0, t: 2 }, { v: 1.6, t: 2 }, { v: -112.3, t: 2 }, { v: 340.8, t: 2 }],
[stderr] +  ]);

[stderr] +  sheet.getRange('A1:F1').setNumberFormats(patterns);
[stderr] +  sheet.getRange('A2:F2').setNumberFormats([['##0', '##0', '##0', '##0', '##0', '##0']]);

[stderr] +      sheet.getRange('A4').setValue({ v: 230, t: 2 });
[stderr] +      sheet.getRange('A4').setNumberFormats([['General']]);
[stderr] +      return { whole: sheet.getRange('A4').getValue(), formula: sheet.getRange('D3').getValue() };
[stderr] +    })(),
[stderr] +    cells: sheet.getRange('A1:F2').getCellDatas(),
[stderr] +  };

[stderr] diff --git a/work/sort_blocks.js b/work/sort_blocks.js
[stderr] new file mode 100644
[stderr] index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..c293033ba9c0f7a348aa21441caf312687023c66
[stderr] --- /dev/null
[stderr] +++ b/work/sort_blocks.js
[stderr] @@ -0,0 +1,140 @@
[stderr] +async () => {
[stderr] +  const workbook = univerAPI.getActiveWorkbook();
[stderr] +  const sheet = workbook.getSheetByName('Sheet1');
[stderr] +  if (!sheet) return { success: false, error: 'Sheet1 not found' };
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const target = sheet.getRange('A1:H85');
[stderr] +  const cellDatas = target.getCellDatas();
[stderr] +  const values = target.getValues();
[stderr] +  const formulas = target.getFormulas();
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const cloneCell = (cell) => {
[stderr] +    if (!cell) return null;
[stderr] +    return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(cell));
[stderr] +  };
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const cleanText = (v) =>
[stderr] +    String(v ?? '').replace(/\u00A0/g, ' ').replace(/\r\n/g, '\n').replace(/\r/g, '\n').trim();
[stderr] +  const isBlankRow = (row) => row.every((cell) => {
[stderr] +    if (!cell) return true;
[stderr] +    if (cell.f) return false;
[stderr] +    if (cell.p?.body?.dataStream) return cleanText(cell.p.body.dataStream) === '';
[stderr] +    return cleanText(cell.v) === '';
[stderr] +  });
[stderr] +  const parseNumberLoose = (v) => {
[stderr] +    const s = cleanText(v).replace(/,/g, '');
[stderr] +    if (s === '') return null;
[stderr] +    const n = Number(s);
[stderr] +    return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null;
[stderr] +  };
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const blocks = [];
[stderr] +  let start = null;
[stderr] +  for (let r = 0; r < 85; r++) {
[stderr] +    const blank = isBlankRow(cellDatas[r]);
[stderr] +    if (!blank && start === null) start = r;
[stderr] +    if ((blank || r === 84) && start !== null) {
[stderr] +      const end = blank ? r - 1 : r;
[stderr] +      blocks.push({ start, end });
[stderr] +      start = null;
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +  }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const out = cellDatas.map((row) => row.map(cloneCell));
[stderr] +  const sortedSummaries = [];
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  for (const block of blocks) {
[stderr] +    const rows = [];
[stderr] +    for (let r = block.start; r <= block.end; r++) {
[stderr] +      rows.push({
[stderr] +        originalIndex: r,
[stderr] +        cells: cellDatas[r].map(cloneCell),
[stderr] +        key: parseNumberLoose(values[r][3]),
[stderr] +      });
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +    rows.sort((a, b) => {
[stderr] +      const ak = a.key;
[stderr] +      const bk = b.key;
[stderr] +      if (ak === null && bk === null) return a.originalIndex - b.originalIndex;
[stderr] +      if (ak === null) return 1;
[stderr] +      if (bk === null) return -1;
[stderr] +      if (bk !== ak) return bk - ak;
[stderr] +      return a.originalIndex - b.originalIndex;
[stderr] +    });
[stderr] +
[stderr] +    for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
[stderr] +      const dest = block.start + i;
[stderr] +      const excelRow = dest + 1;
[stderr] +      const rowCells = rows[i].cells.map(cloneCell);
[stderr] +
[stderr] +      for (let c = 0; c < 8; c++) {
[stderr] +        const cell = rowCells[c];
[stderr] +        if (!cell) continue;
[stderr] +        if (!cell.f && typeof cell.v === 'string' && cleanText(cell.v).startsWith('=')) {
[stderr] +          cell.f = cleanText(cell.v);
[stderr] +          delete cell.v;
[stderr] +          delete cell.t;
[stderr] +        }
[stderr] +      }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +      rowCells[3] = { ...(rowCells[3] || {}), f: `=B${excelRow}+C${excelRow}` };
[stderr] +      rowCells[5] = { ...(rowCells[5] || {}), f: `=D${excelRow}+E${excelRow}` };
[stderr] +      delete rowCells[3].si;
[stderr] +      delete rowCells[5].si;
[stderr] +
[stderr] +      out[dest] = rowCells;
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +    sortedSummaries.push({
[stderr] +      rows: `${block.start + 1}:${block.end + 1}`,
[stderr] +      keys: rows.map((r) => r.key),
[stderr] +    });
[stderr] +  }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  target.setValues(out);
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const numberFormats = Array.from({ length: 85 }, () => ['##0.#', '##0.#', '##0.#']);
[stderr] +  sheet.getRange('D1:F85')
[stderr] +    .setFontWeight('bold')
[stderr] +    .setHorizontalAlignment('center')
[stderr] +    .setNumberFormats(numberFormats);
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  await univerAPI.getFormula().onCalculationResultApplied();
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const after = sheet.getRange('A1:H85');
[stderr] +  const afterValues = after.getValues();
[stderr] +  const afterFormulas = after.getFormulas();
[stderr] +  const afterCells = after.getCellDatas();
[stderr] +  const blockChecks = blocks.map((block) => {
[stderr] +    const keys = [];
[stderr] +    const formulasOk = [];
[stderr] +    for (let r = block.start; r <= block.end; r++) {
[stderr] +      keys.push(parseNumberLoose(afterValues[r][3]));
[stderr] +      formulasOk.push(afterFormulas[r][3] === `=B${r + 1}+C${r + 1}` && afterFormulas[r][5] === `=D${r + 1}+E${r + 1}`);
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +    return {
[stderr] +      rows: `${block.start + 1}:${block.end + 1}`,
[stderr] +      descending: keys.every((v, i) => i === 0 || keys[i - 1] >= v),
[stderr] +      formulasOk: formulasOk.every(Boolean),
[stderr] +      keys,
[stderr] +    };
[stderr] +  });
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const blankRows = [];
[stderr] +  for (let r = 0; r < 85; r++) {
[stderr] +    if (isBlankRow(afterCells[r])) blankRows.push(r + 1);
[stderr] +  }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  return {
[stderr] +    success: true,
[stderr] +    blocks: blocks.map((b) => `${b.start + 1}:${b.end + 1}`),
[stderr] +    blankRows,
[stderr] +    sortedSummaries,
[stderr] +    blockChecks,
[stderr] +    samples: [
[stderr] +      { row: 1, values: afterValues[0], formulas: afterFormulas[0], cells: afterCells[0].slice(3, 6) },
[stderr] +      { row: 8, cells: afterCells[7] },
[stderr] +      { row: 85, values: afterValues[84], formulas: afterFormulas[84], cells: afterCells[84].slice(3, 6) },
[stderr] +    ],
[stderr] +  };
[stderr] +}

[stderr] +  const workbook = univerAPI.getActiveWorkbook();
[stderr] +  const sheet = workbook.getSheetByName('Sheet1');
[stderr] +  if (!sheet) return { success: false, error: 'Sheet1 not found' };
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const range = sheet.getRange('A1:H85');
[stderr] +  const values = range.getValues();
[stderr] +  const formulas = range.getFormulas();
[stderr] +  const cellDatas = range.getCellDatas();
[stderr] +  const cleanText = (v) => String(v ?? '').replace(/\u00A0/g, ' ').trim();
[stderr] +  const parseNumberLoose = (v) => {
[stderr] +    const s = cleanText(v).replace(/,/g, '');
[stderr] +    if (s === '') return null;
[stderr] +    const n = Number(s);
[stderr] +    return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null;
[stderr] +  };
[stderr] +  const isBlankRow = (row) => row.every((cell) => !cell || (!cell.f && cleanText(cell.v) === ''));
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const blocks = [];
[stderr] +  let start = null;
[stderr] +  for (let r = 0; r < 85; r++) {
[stderr] +    const blank = isBlankRow(cellDatas[r]);
[stderr] +    if (!blank && start === null) start = r;
[stderr] +    if ((blank || r === 84) && start !== null) {
[stderr] +      blocks.push({ start, end: blank ? r - 1 : r });
[stderr] +      start = null;
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +  }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const checks = blocks.map((b) => {
[stderr] +    const keys = [];
[stderr] +    const formulaProblems = [];
[stderr] +    const textFormulaProblems = [];
[stderr] +    for (let r = b.start; r <= b.end; r++) {
[stderr] +      keys.push(parseNumberLoose(values[r][3]));
[stderr] +      if (formulas[r][3] !== `=B${r + 1}+C${r + 1}`) formulaProblems.push(`D${r + 1}:${formulas[r][3]}`);
[stderr] +      if (formulas[r][5] !== `=D${r + 1}+E${r + 1}`) formulaProblems.push(`F${r + 1}:${formulas[r][5]}`);
[stderr] +      for (const c of [3, 4, 5]) {
[stderr] +        const cell = cellDatas[r][c];
[stderr] +        if (typeof cell?.v === 'string' && cleanText(cell.v).startsWith('=')) {
[stderr] +          textFormulaProblems.push(`${String.fromCharCode(65 + c)}${r + 1}`);
[stderr] +        }
[stderr] +      }
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +    return {
[stderr] +      rows: `${b.start + 1}:${b.end + 1}`,
[stderr] +      descending: keys.every((v, i) => i === 0 || keys[i - 1] >= v),
[stderr] +      formulaProblems,
[stderr] +      textFormulaProblems,
[stderr] +      keys,
[stderr] +    };
[stderr] +  });
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const display = sheet.getRange('D1:F5').getValues();
[stderr] +  const sampleCells = [0, 1, 7, 8, 84].map((r) => ({
[stderr] +    row: r + 1,
[stderr] +    values: values[r],
[stderr] +    formulas: formulas[r],
[stderr] +    dToF: cellDatas[r].slice(3, 6),
[stderr] +  }));
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  return {
[stderr] +    success: true,
[stderr] +    blocks: blocks.map((b) => `${b.start + 1}:${b.end + 1}`),
[stderr] +    blankRows: cellDatas.map((row, i) => isBlankRow(row) ? i + 1 : null).filter(Boolean),
[stderr] +    allDescending: checks.every((c) => c.descending),
[stderr] +    allFormulasOk: checks.every((c) => c.formulaProblems.length === 0),
[stderr] +    noTextStoredFormulasInDToF: checks.every((c) => c.textFormulaProblems.length === 0),
[stderr] +    checks,
[stderr] +    displayD1F5: display,
[stderr] +    sampleCells,
[stderr] +  };
[stderr] +}

[stderr] +      delete rowCells[5].si;
[stderr] +
[stderr] +      out[dest] = rowCells;
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +    sortedSummaries.push({
[stderr] +      rows: `${block.start + 1}:${block.end + 1}`,
[stderr] +      keys: rows.map((r) => r.key),
[stderr] +    });
[stderr] +  }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  target.setValues(out);
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const numberFormats = Array.from({ length: 85 }, () => ['##0.#', '##0.#', '##0.#']);
[stderr] +  sheet.getRange('D1:F85')
[stderr] +    .setFontWeight('bold')
[stderr] +    .setHorizontalAlignment('center')
[stderr] +    .setNumberFormats(numberFormats);
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  await univerAPI.getFormula().onCalculationResultApplied();
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const after = sheet.getRange('A1:H85');
[stderr] +  const afterValues = after.getValues();
[stderr] +  const afterFormulas = after.getFormulas();
[stderr] +  const afterCells = after.getCellDatas();
[stderr] +  const blockChecks = blocks.map((block) => {
[stderr] +    const keys = [];
[stderr] +    const formulasOk = [];
[stderr] +    for (let r = block.start; r <= block.end; r++) {
[stderr] +      keys.push(parseNumberLoose(afterValues[r][3]));
[stderr] +      formulasOk.push(afterFormulas[r][3] === `=B${r + 1}+C${r + 1}` && afterFormulas[r][5] === `=D${r + 1}+E${r + 1}`);
[stderr] +    }
[stderr] +    return {
[stderr] +      rows: `${block.start + 1}:${block.end + 1}`,
[stderr] +      descending: keys.every((v, i) => i === 0 || keys[i - 1] >= v),
[stderr] +      formulasOk: formulasOk.every(Boolean),
[stderr] +      keys,
[stderr] +    };
[stderr] +  });
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  const blankRows = [];
[stderr] +  for (let r = 0; r < 85; r++) {
[stderr] +    if (isBlankRow(afterCells[r])) blankRows.push(r + 1);
[stderr] +  }
[stderr] +
[stderr] +  return {
[stderr] +    success: true,
[stderr] +    blocks: blocks.map((b) => `${b.start + 1}:${b.end + 1}`),
[stderr] +    blankRows,
[stderr] +    sortedSummaries,
[stderr] +    blockChecks,
[stderr] +    samples: [
[stderr] +      { row: 1, values: afterValues[0], formulas: afterFormulas[0], cells: afterCells[0].slice(3, 6) },
[stderr] +      { row: 8, cells: afterCells[7] },
[stderr] +      { row: 85, values: afterValues[84], formulas: afterFormulas[84], cells: afterCells[84].slice(3, 6) },
[stderr] +    ],
[stderr] +  };

[stderr] +() => {

[stderr] +  if (!sheet) return { success: false, error: 'Sheet1 not found' };
[stderr] +
